### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP と UDP の一番の違いは、**TCP は届いたか確認して順番どおりに届ける信頼性重視の方式、UDP は確認や順序保証をせずに速く送る方式**だという点です。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para actualizar o limpiar tu rama privada, que nadie más usa todavía: así el historial queda lineal. Usa **merge** cuando la rama ya es compartida o pública, porque reescribir su historial rompería el trabajo de otros.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的，保证数据有序、不丢不重地送达，但要先建立连接、还要重传确认，速度相对慢；UDP 不保证可靠和顺序，但开销小、延迟低，适合视频、语音、游戏这类对实时性要求高的场景。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared feature branch to keep history linear and tidy it up before review. Merge when the branch is shared with others or you want the record of when work actually joined, since rebasing published commits forces people to reconcile their copies.
```

背景：用户周六（11月28日）去北京 agibar 参加晚上活动，当晚住一晚，周日（11月29日）回去。活动地址：北京市海淀区中关村创业大街2号楼A号底商。

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a log file on disk before the change is applied to the actual data, so a database can recover after a crash by replaying committed entries and discarding incomplete ones. Because the log is written sequentially, it also makes commits fast, since only the log has to be flushed to disk, not every modified data page.
```

    var tv = document.getElementById('total');

    document.getElementById('sub').textContent = n
      ? state.days.length + ' 天,' + n + ' 笔'
      : '按天记,合计自动算';
    ducter.summary(n ? n + ' 笔,合计 ' + fmt(total) : '还没记');
  }

  document.getElementById('addDay').onclick = function(){
    var name = document.getElementById('dayName').value.trim() || ('第 ' + (state.days.length + 1) + ' 天');
    state.days.push({label: name, items: []});
    document.getElementById('dayName').value = '';
    save();
  };

  ducter.onChange(function(){
    var v = ducter.get('trip');
    if (v && v.days){ state = v; render(); }
  });

  render();
})();

.hb-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(7,1fr);gap:5px;margin-top:12px}
.hb-day{aspect-ratio:1;border-radius:6px;background:var(--d-sunken);border:1px solid var(--d-line);color:var(--d-muted);font-size:12px;padding:0;cursor:pointer}

</style>
<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Today</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="today">0 / 3</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sumsub">Tick what you did today</div>
</section>
<div id="cards"></div>
</main>
<script>
const HABITS = ducter.get("habits") || [];
const WINDOW = 28;
let data = {};

const pad = n => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
const key = d => d.getFullYear() + '-' +  … ap(h => {
    const isDone = done(h.id, t);
    return `<section class="k-card hb-card">
      <div class="k-h">${h.name}</div>
      <div class="k-row-sub">${h.sub}</div>
      <div class="hb-stats">
        <div><b>${streak(h.id)}</b>day streak</div>
        <div><b>${best(h.id)}</b>best</div>
        <div><b>${countWindow(h.id, days)}</b>of last ${WINDOW}</div>
      </div>
      <div class="hb-grid">${days.map(d => {
        const k = key(d);
        const cls = ['hb-day', done(h.id, k) ? 'on' : '', k === t ? 'today' : ''].join(' ');
        return `<button class="${cls}" data-h="${h.id}" data-k="${k}" aria-label="${k}">${d.getDate()}</button>`;
      }).join('')}</div>
      <div class="hb-foot">Tap any day to fix it</div>
      <button class="k-btn primary hb-tick ${isDone ? 'on' : ''}" data-h="${h.id}" data-k="${t}">${isDone ? 'Done today ✓' : 'Tick today'}</button>
    </section>`;
  }).join('');
}

document.getElementById('cards').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('[data-h]');
  if (b) toggle(b.dataset.h, b.dataset.k);
});

async function load() {
  data = (await ducter.get('ticks')) || {};
  render();
}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] The PR page is ready. It lists your four open PRs, and tapping one ticks it off as it lands. The count at the top drops as you go.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on others", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2633 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Still waiting</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">4</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tap a PR to tick it off when it lands</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">My open PRs</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var PRS = ducter.get("prs") || [];
  var done = {};
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function save() {
    try { ducter.set('done', done); } catch (e) {}
  }

  function render() {
    list.innerHTML = '';
    var left = 0;
    PRS.forEach(function (p) {
      var isDone = !!done[p.id];
      if (!isDone) left++;
      var li = document.createElement('li');
      li.className = 'k-row';
      li.setAttribute('role', 'button');
      li.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isDone));
      li.style.cursor = 'pointer';

      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      var box = document.createElement('span');
      box.textContent = isDone ? '☑ ' : '☐ ';
      var title = docu … 
      main.appendChild(title);

      var sub1 = document.createElement('div');
      sub1.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub1.textContent = isDone ? 'Landed' : p.status;
      if (!isDone && p.bad) sub1.style.color = 'var(--d-bad)';

      li.appendChild(main);
      li.appendChild(sub1);

      li.addEventListener('click', function () {
        if (done[p.id]) { delete done[p.id]; } else { done[p.id] = true; }
        save();
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(li);
    });
    count.textContent = String(left);
    sub.textContent = left === 0 ? 'All landed' : 'Tap a PR to tick it off when it lands';
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
      try { ducter.summary(left + ' waiting'); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }

  if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined') {
    try {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) {
        done = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
        render();
      });
    } catch (e) { render(); }
    try { ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('done')).then(function (v) { done = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {}; render(); }); }); } catch (e) {}
  } else {
    render();
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"prs":[{"id":"ducter-412","ref":"wey-gu/ducter #412","what":"Compaction cut","status":"Waiting on Weizhen’s review · since Mon","bad":false},{"id":"mem-10287","ref":"nowledge-co/mem #10287","what":"Retry on 502","status":"CI failing","bad":true},{"id":"hawdb-1592","ref":"nowledge-co/hawdb #1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","status":"Waiting on Alice · since Tue","bad":false},{"id":"ducter-418","ref":"wey-gu/ducter #418","what":"Apps bar","status":"Draft","bad":false}]}

```

(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.

  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

要求：
- 在现有的 TTL 测试（已改成假时钟）旁边再加一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好到期那一刻的行为。
- 用假时钟把时间推进到正好等于过期时刻：此时值应当已经过期（或按现有约定处理边界），并补一条刚好早于到期时刻仍然有效的断言，把边界两侧都锁住。
- 不要用真实 sleep 或真实计时。
- 完成标准：新测试通过，且把整个 TTL 测试文件连续跑 20 次都通过，把输出结果报告给我。
- 范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR，改动留在现有分支上。
```
